-void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
-                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
-                   const size_t numNodes) {
-    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
-    // For each intermediate node k
+void updateNextPivot(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                     std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
+                     const std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotRow,
+                     const std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotColumn,
+                     const size_t localRows, const size_t localColumns,
+                     const size_t nextLocalRow, const size_t nextLocalColumn,
+                     const unsigned int k) noexcept {
+    if (localColumns == 0U) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    if (nextLocalRow < localRows) {
+        unsigned int* const distRow =
+            dist.data() + nextLocalRow * localColumns;
+        unsigned int* const pathRow =
+            path.data() + nextLocalRow * localColumns;
+        updateRange(distRow, pathRow, pivotColumn[nextLocalRow], pivotRow.data(),
+                    0U, localColumns, k);
+    }
+
+    if (nextLocalColumn < localColumns) {
+        for (size_t row = 0; row < localRows; ++row) {
+            if (row == nextLocalRow) {
+                continue;
+            }
+            unsigned int& oldDistance =
+                dist[idx2(row, nextLocalColumn, localColumns)];
+            const unsigned int newDistance =
+                pivotColumn[row] + pivotRow[nextLocalColumn];
+            if (newDistance < oldDistance) {
+                oldDistance = newDistance;
+                path[idx2(row, nextLocalColumn, localColumns)] = k;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void preparePivot(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                  std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotRow,
+                  std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotColumn,
+                  const size_t k, const size_t rowStart,
+                  const size_t localRows, const size_t columnStart,
+                  const size_t localColumns) {
+    if (localColumns != 0U && k >= rowStart &&
+        k < rowStart + localRows) {
+        const unsigned int* const source =
+            dist.data() + (k - rowStart) * localColumns;
+        std::copy_n(source, localColumns, pivotRow.data());
+    }
+    if (k >= columnStart && k < columnStart + localColumns) {
+        const size_t localColumn = k - columnStart;
+        for (size_t row = 0; row < localRows; ++row) {
+            pivotColumn[row] = dist[idx2(row, localColumn, localColumns)];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void startPivotBroadcast(std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotRow,
+                         std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotColumn,
+                         const size_t k, const size_t numNodes,
+                         const int processRows, const int processColumns,
+                         MPI_Comm columnComm, MPI_Comm rowComm,
+                         MPI_Request requests[2]) {
+    const int pivotProcessRow = blockOwner(k, numNodes, processRows);
+    const int pivotProcessColumn = blockOwner(k, numNodes, processColumns);
+    MPI_Ibcast(pivotRow.data(), static_cast<int>(pivotRow.size()), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+               pivotProcessRow, columnComm, &requests[0]);
+    MPI_Ibcast(pivotColumn.data(), static_cast<int>(pivotColumn.size()),
+               MPI_UNSIGNED, pivotProcessColumn, rowComm, &requests[1]);
+}
+
+void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
+                   const size_t numNodes, const size_t rowStart,
+                   const size_t localRows, const size_t columnStart,
+                   const size_t localColumns, const int processRows,
+                   const int processColumns, MPI_Comm columnComm,
+                   MPI_Comm rowComm) {
+    if (numNodes == 0U) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotRows[2] = {
+        std::vector<unsigned int>(localColumns),
+        std::vector<unsigned int>(localColumns)};
+    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotColumns[2] = {
+        std::vector<unsigned int>(localRows),
+        std::vector<unsigned int>(localRows)};
+    MPI_Request requests[2][2];
+
+    int slot = 0;
+    preparePivot(dist, pivotRows[slot], pivotColumns[slot], 0U, rowStart,
+                 localRows, columnStart, localColumns);
+    startPivotBroadcast(pivotRows[slot], pivotColumns[slot], 0U, numNodes,
+                        processRows, processColumns, columnComm, rowComm,
+                        requests[slot]);
+
     for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
-        // For each source node i
-        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
-            // For each destination node j
-            for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
-                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
-                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
-                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
-                
-                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distKJ;
-                
-                if (newDist < distIJ) {
-                    dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = newDist;
-                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
+        MPI_Waitall(2, requests[slot], MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+
+        if (k + 1U < numNodes) {
+            const size_t nextK = k + 1U;
+            const size_t nextLocalRow =
+                nextK >= rowStart && nextK < rowStart + localRows
+                    ? nextK - rowStart
+                    : localRows;
+            const size_t nextLocalColumn =
+                nextK >= columnStart && nextK < columnStart + localColumns
+                    ? nextK - columnStart
+                    : localColumns;
+
+            // Update the row and column needed by iteration k+1 first.  Their
+            // broadcasts can then progress while the rest of this block is
+            // computed.
+            updateNextPivot(dist, path, pivotRows[slot], pivotColumns[slot],
+                            localRows, localColumns, nextLocalRow,
+                            nextLocalColumn, static_cast<unsigned int>(k));
+
+            const int nextSlot = slot ^ 1;
+            preparePivot(dist, pivotRows[nextSlot], pivotColumns[nextSlot],
+                         nextK, rowStart, localRows, columnStart, localColumns);
+            startPivotBroadcast(pivotRows[nextSlot], pivotColumns[nextSlot],
+                                nextK, numNodes, processRows, processColumns,
+                                columnComm, rowComm, requests[nextSlot]);
+
+            updateBlock(dist, path, pivotRows[slot], pivotColumns[slot],
+                        localRows, localColumns, nextLocalRow, nextLocalColumn,
+                        static_cast<unsigned int>(k));
+            slot = nextSlot;
+        } else {
+            updateBlock(dist, path, pivotRows[slot], pivotColumns[slot],
+                        localRows, localColumns, localRows, localColumns,
+                        static_cast<unsigned int>(k));
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+std::vector<unsigned int> gatherDistanceMatrix(
+    const std::vector<unsigned int>& localDist, const size_t numNodes,
+    const int worldRank, const int worldSize, const int processRows,
+    const int processColumns, MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr int gatherTag = 417;
+    std::vector<unsigned int> result;
+    if (worldRank == 0) {
+        result.resize(numNodes * numNodes);
+    }
+
+    for (int sender = 0; sender < worldSize; ++sender) {
+        const int senderRow = sender / processColumns;
+        const int senderColumn = sender % processColumns;
+        const size_t senderRows = blockSize(numNodes, senderRow, processRows);
+        const size_t senderColumns =
+            blockSize(numNodes, senderColumn, processColumns);
+        const size_t senderRowStart =
+            blockStart(numNodes, senderRow, processRows);
+        const size_t senderColumnStart =
+            blockStart(numNodes, senderColumn, processColumns);
+
+        if (senderRows == 0U || senderColumns == 0U) {
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        const size_t rowsPerMessage =
+            static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX) / senderColumns;
+        for (size_t rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < senderRows;
+             rowOffset += rowsPerMessage) {
+            const size_t messageRows =
+                std::min(rowsPerMessage, senderRows - rowOffset);
+            const int messageElements =
+                static_cast<int>(messageRows * senderColumns);
+
+            if (worldRank == sender) {
+                if (sender == 0) {
+                    for (size_t row = 0; row < messageRows; ++row) {
+                        const unsigned int* const source =
+                            localDist.data() +
+                            (rowOffset + row) * senderColumns;
+                        unsigned int* const destination =
+                            result.data() +
+                            (senderRowStart + rowOffset + row) * numNodes +
+                            senderColumnStart;
+                        std::copy_n(source, senderColumns, destination);
+                    }
+                } else {
+                    MPI_Send(localDist.data() + rowOffset * senderColumns,
+                             messageElements, MPI_UNSIGNED, 0, gatherTag,
+                             communicator);
                 }
+            } else if (worldRank == 0) {
+                const int globalSizes[2] = {static_cast<int>(numNodes),
+                                            static_cast<int>(numNodes)};
+                const int subSizes[2] = {static_cast<int>(messageRows),
+                                         static_cast<int>(senderColumns)};
+                const int starts[2] = {
+                    static_cast<int>(senderRowStart + rowOffset),
+                    static_cast<int>(senderColumnStart)};
+                MPI_Datatype receiveType;
+                MPI_Type_create_subarray(2, globalSizes, subSizes, starts,
+                                         MPI_ORDER_C, MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                                         &receiveType);
+                MPI_Type_commit(&receiveType);
+                MPI_Recv(result.data(), 1, receiveType, sender, gatherTag,
+                         communicator, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                MPI_Type_free(&receiveType);
             }
         }
     }
+
+    return result;
 }

+bool parseNodeCount(const char* text, size_t& value) noexcept {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' || text[0] == '-' ||
+        parsed > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max()) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
 }

+            if (worldRank == 0) {

+                valid = validateResult(distanceMatrix, numNodes) ? 1 : 0;
+                std::printf("Validation: %s\n", valid != 0 ? "PASSED" : "FAILED");
+            }
+            MPI_Bcast(&valid, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+            returnCode = valid != 0 ? 0 : 1;
+        }
+    } catch (const std::exception& exception) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d failed: %s\n", worldRank,
+                     exception.what());
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        returnCode = 1;
     }
-    
-    return 0;
+
+    MPI_Comm_free(&columnComm);
+    MPI_Comm_free(&rowComm);
+    MPI_Finalize();
+    return returnCode;
 }

 constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;

-// Index calculation for flattened 2D array
-inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
-    return j * n + i;
+// Index calculation for a flattened, row-major 2D array.
+inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t row, const size_t column,
+                             const size_t rowLength) noexcept {
+    return row * rowLength + column;
+}
+
+inline size_t blockSize(const size_t n, const int coordinate,
+                        const int parts) noexcept {
+    const size_t base = n / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t remainder = n % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    return base + (static_cast<size_t>(coordinate) < remainder ? 1U : 0U);
+}
+
+inline size_t blockStart(const size_t n, const int coordinate,
+                         const int parts) noexcept {
+    const size_t base = n / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t remainder = n % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    return static_cast<size_t>(coordinate) * base +
+           std::min(static_cast<size_t>(coordinate), remainder);
+}
+
+inline int blockOwner(const size_t index, const size_t n,
+                      const int parts) noexcept {
+    const size_t base = n / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t remainder = n % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t largeBlockElements = (base + 1U) * remainder;
+
+    if (index < largeBlockElements) {
+        return static_cast<int>(index / (base + 1U));
+    }
+    return static_cast<int>(remainder +
+                            (index - largeBlockElements) / base);
+}
+
+// glibc rand_r advances its 32-bit LCG three times per returned value.  Jumping
+// that LCG ahead lets every process initialize its own matrix block while
+// reproducing exactly the single-process benchmark's seed-42 input matrix.
+unsigned int advanceRandomSeed(const unsigned int initialSeed,
+                               const uint64_t randomValues) noexcept {
+    uint64_t delta = randomValues * 3U;
+    uint32_t accumulatedMultiplier = 1U;
+    uint32_t accumulatedIncrement = 0U;
+    uint32_t currentMultiplier = 1103515245U;
+    uint32_t currentIncrement = 12345U;
+
+    while (delta != 0U) {
+        if ((delta & 1U) != 0U) {
+            accumulatedMultiplier = static_cast<uint32_t>(
+                static_cast<uint64_t>(accumulatedMultiplier) * currentMultiplier);
+            accumulatedIncrement = static_cast<uint32_t>(
+                static_cast<uint64_t>(accumulatedIncrement) * currentMultiplier +
+                currentIncrement);
+        }
+        currentIncrement = static_cast<uint32_t>(
+            (static_cast<uint64_t>(currentMultiplier) + 1U) * currentIncrement);
+        currentMultiplier = static_cast<uint32_t>(
+            static_cast<uint64_t>(currentMultiplier) * currentMultiplier);
+        delta >>= 1U;
+    }
+
+    return static_cast<unsigned int>(
+        static_cast<uint32_t>(
+            static_cast<uint64_t>(accumulatedMultiplier) * initialSeed +
+            accumulatedIncrement));
 }

+inline void updateRange(unsigned int* const distRow,

-void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
-                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
-                   const size_t numNodes) {
-    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
-    // For each intermediate node k
+void updateNextPivot(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                     std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
+                     const std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotRow,
+                     const std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotColumn,
+                     const size_t localRows, const size_t localColumns,
+                     const size_t nextLocalRow, const size_t nextLocalColumn,
+                     const unsigned int k) noexcept {
+    if (localColumns == 0U) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    if (nextLocalRow < localRows) {
+        unsigned int* const distRow =
+            dist.data() + nextLocalRow * localColumns;
+        unsigned int* const pathRow =
+            path.data() + nextLocalRow * localColumns;
+        updateRange(distRow, pathRow, pivotColumn[nextLocalRow], pivotRow.data(),
+                    0U, localColumns, k);
+    }
+
+    if (nextLocalColumn < localColumns) {
+        for (size_t row = 0; row < localRows; ++row) {
+            if (row == nextLocalRow) {
+                continue;
+            }
+            unsigned int& oldDistance =
+                dist[idx2(row, nextLocalColumn, localColumns)];
+            const unsigned int newDistance =
+                pivotColumn[row] + pivotRow[nextLocalColumn];
+            if (newDistance < oldDistance) {
+                oldDistance = newDistance;
+                path[idx2(row, nextLocalColumn, localColumns)] = k;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void preparePivot(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                  std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotRow,
+                  std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotColumn,
+                  const size_t k, const size_t rowStart,
+                  const size_t localRows, const size_t columnStart,
+                  const size_t localColumns) {
+    if (localColumns != 0U && k >= rowStart &&
+        k < rowStart + localRows) {
+        const unsigned int* const source =
+            dist.data() + (k - rowStart) * localColumns;
+        std::copy_n(source, localColumns, pivotRow.data());
+    }
+    if (k >= columnStart && k < columnStart + localColumns) {
+        const size_t localColumn = k - columnStart;
+        for (size_t row = 0; row < localRows; ++row) {
+            pivotColumn[row] = dist[idx2(row, localColumn, localColumns)];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void startPivotBroadcast(std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotRow,
+                         std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotColumn,
+                         const size_t k, const size_t numNodes,
+                         const int processRows, const int processColumns,
+                         MPI_Comm columnComm, MPI_Comm rowComm,
+                         MPI_Request requests[2]) {
+    const int pivotProcessRow = blockOwner(k, numNodes, processRows);
+    const int pivotProcessColumn = blockOwner(k, numNodes, processColumns);
+    MPI_Ibcast(pivotRow.data(), static_cast<int>(pivotRow.size()), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+               pivotProcessRow, columnComm, &requests[0]);
+    MPI_Ibcast(pivotColumn.data(), static_cast<int>(pivotColumn.size()),
+               MPI_UNSIGNED, pivotProcessColumn, rowComm, &requests[1]);
+}
+
+void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
+                   const size_t numNodes, const size_t rowStart,
+                   const size_t localRows, const size_t columnStart,
+                   const size_t localColumns, const int processRows,
+                   const int processColumns, MPI_Comm columnComm,
+                   MPI_Comm rowComm) {
+    if (numNodes == 0U) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotRows[2] = {
+        std::vector<unsigned int>(localColumns),
+        std::vector<unsigned int>(localColumns)};
+    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotColumns[2] = {
+        std::vector<unsigned int>(localRows),
+        std::vector<unsigned int>(localRows)};
+    MPI_Request requests[2][2];
+
+    int slot = 0;
+    preparePivot(dist, pivotRows[slot], pivotColumns[slot], 0U, rowStart,
+                 localRows, columnStart, localColumns);
+    startPivotBroadcast(pivotRows[slot], pivotColumns[slot], 0U, numNodes,
+                        processRows, processColumns, columnComm, rowComm,
+                        requests[slot]);
+
     for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
-        // For each source node i
-        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
-            // For each destination node j
-            for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
-                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
-                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
-                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
-                
-                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distKJ;
-                
-                if (newDist < distIJ) {
-                    dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = newDist;
-                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
+        MPI_Waitall(2, requests[slot], MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+
+        if (k + 1U < numNodes) {
+            const size_t nextK = k + 1U;
+            const size_t nextLocalRow =
+                nextK >= rowStart && nextK < rowStart + localRows
+                    ? nextK - rowStart
+                    : localRows;
+            const size_t nextLocalColumn =
+                nextK >= columnStart && nextK < columnStart + localColumns
+                    ? nextK - columnStart
+                    : localColumns;
+
+            // Update the row and column needed by iteration k+1 first.  Their
+            // broadcasts can then progress while the rest of this block is
+            // computed.
+            updateNextPivot(dist, path, pivotRows[slot], pivotColumns[slot],
+                            localRows, localColumns, nextLocalRow,
+                            nextLocalColumn, static_cast<unsigned int>(k));
+
+            const int nextSlot = slot ^ 1;
+            preparePivot(dist, pivotRows[nextSlot], pivotColumns[nextSlot],
+                         nextK, rowStart, localRows, columnStart, localColumns);
+            startPivotBroadcast(pivotRows[nextSlot], pivotColumns[nextSlot],
+                                nextK, numNodes, processRows, processColumns,
+                                columnComm, rowComm, requests[nextSlot]);
+
+            updateBlock(dist, path, pivotRows[slot], pivotColumns[slot],
+                        localRows, localColumns, nextLocalRow, nextLocalColumn,
+                        static_cast<unsigned int>(k));
+            slot = nextSlot;
+        } else {
+            updateBlock(dist, path, pivotRows[slot], pivotColumns[slot],
+                        localRows, localColumns, localRows, localColumns,
+                        static_cast<unsigned int>(k));
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+std::vector<unsigned int> gatherDistanceMatrix(
+    const std::vector<unsigned int>& localDist, const size_t numNodes,
+    const int worldRank, const int worldSize, const int processRows,
+    const int processColumns, MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr int gatherTag = 417;
+    std::vector<unsigned int> result;
+    if (worldRank == 0) {
+        result.resize(numNodes * numNodes);
+    }
+
+    for (int sender = 0; sender < worldSize; ++sender) {
+        const int senderRow = sender / processColumns;
+        const int senderColumn = sender % processColumns;
+        const size_t senderRows = blockSize(numNodes, senderRow, processRows);
+        const size_t senderColumns =
+            blockSize(numNodes, senderColumn, processColumns);
+        const size_t senderRowStart =
+            blockStart(numNodes, senderRow, processRows);
+        const size_t senderColumnStart =
+            blockStart(numNodes, senderColumn, processColumns);
+
+        if (senderRows == 0U || senderColumns == 0U) {
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        const size_t rowsPerMessage =
+            static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX) / senderColumns;
+        for (size_t rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < senderRows;
+             rowOffset += rowsPerMessage) {
+            const size_t messageRows =
+                std::min(rowsPerMessage, senderRows - rowOffset);
+            const int messageElements =
+                static_cast<int>(messageRows * senderColumns);
+
+            if (worldRank == sender) {
+                if (sender == 0) {
+                    for (size_t row = 0; row < messageRows; ++row) {
+                        const unsigned int* const source =
+                            localDist.data() +
+                            (rowOffset + row) * senderColumns;
+                        unsigned int* const destination =
+                            result.data() +
+                            (senderRowStart + rowOffset + row) * numNodes +
+                            senderColumnStart;
+                        std::copy_n(source, senderColumns, destination);
+                    }
+                } else {
+                    MPI_Send(localDist.data() + rowOffset * senderColumns,
+                             messageElements, MPI_UNSIGNED, 0, gatherTag,
+                             communicator);
                 }
+            } else if (worldRank == 0) {
+                const int globalSizes[2] = {static_cast<int>(numNodes),
+                                            static_cast<int>(numNodes)};
+                const int subSizes[2] = {static_cast<int>(messageRows),
+                                         static_cast<int>(senderColumns)};
+                const int starts[2] = {
+                    static_cast<int>(senderRowStart + rowOffset),
+                    static_cast<int>(senderColumnStart)};
+                MPI_Datatype receiveType;
+                MPI_Type_create_subarray(2, globalSizes, subSizes, starts,
+                                         MPI_ORDER_C, MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                                         &receiveType);
+                MPI_Type_commit(&receiveType);
+                MPI_Recv(result.data(), 1, receiveType, sender, gatherTag,
+                         communicator, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                MPI_Type_free(&receiveType);
             }
         }
     }
+
+    return result;
 }

+bool parseNodeCount(const char* text, size_t& value) noexcept {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' || text[0] == '-' ||
+        parsed > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max()) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
 }

+// Index calculation for a flattened, row-major 2D array.
+inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t row, const size_t column,
+                             const size_t rowLength) noexcept {
+    return row * rowLength + column;

+
+inline size_t blockSize(const size_t n, const int coordinate,
+                        const int parts) noexcept {
+    const size_t base = n / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t remainder = n % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    return base + (static_cast<size_t>(coordinate) < remainder ? 1U : 0U);
+}
+
+inline size_t blockStart(const size_t n, const int coordinate,
+                         const int parts) noexcept {
+    const size_t base = n / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t remainder = n % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    return static_cast<size_t>(coordinate) * base +
+           std::min(static_cast<size_t>(coordinate), remainder);
+}
+
+inline int blockOwner(const size_t index, const size_t n,
+                      const int parts) noexcept {
+    const size_t base = n / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t remainder = n % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t largeBlockElements = (base + 1U) * remainder;
+
+    if (index < largeBlockElements) {
+        return static_cast<int>(index / (base + 1U));
+    }
+    return static_cast<int>(remainder +
+                            (index - largeBlockElements) / base);
+}
+
+// glibc rand_r advances its 32-bit LCG three times per returned value.  Jumping
+// that LCG ahead lets every process initialize its own matrix block while
+// reproducing exactly the single-process benchmark's seed-42 input matrix.
+unsigned int advanceRandomSeed(const unsigned int initialSeed,
+                               const uint64_t randomValues) noexcept {
+    uint64_t delta = randomValues * 3U;
+    uint32_t accumulatedMultiplier = 1U;
+    uint32_t accumulatedIncrement = 0U;
+    uint32_t currentMultiplier = 1103515245U;
+    uint32_t currentIncrement = 12345U;
+
+    while (delta != 0U) {
+        if ((delta & 1U) != 0U) {
+            accumulatedMultiplier = static_cast<uint32_t>(
+                static_cast<uint64_t>(accumulatedMultiplier) * currentMultiplier);
+            accumulatedIncrement = static_cast<uint32_t>(
+                static_cast<uint64_t>(accumulatedIncrement) * currentMultiplier +
+                currentIncrement);
+        }
+        currentIncrement = static_cast<uint32_t>(
+            (static_cast<uint64_t>(currentMultiplier) + 1U) * currentIncrement);
+        currentMultiplier = static_cast<uint32_t>(
+            static_cast<uint64_t>(currentMultiplier) * currentMultiplier);
+        delta >>= 1U;
+    }
+
+    return static_cast<unsigned int>(
+        static_cast<uint32_t>(
+            static_cast<uint64_t>(accumulatedMultiplier) * initialSeed +
+            accumulatedIncrement));
 }

-void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
-                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
-                   const size_t numNodes) {
-    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
-    // For each intermediate node k
+void updateNextPivot(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                     std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
+                     const std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotRow,
+                     const std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotColumn,
+                     const size_t localRows, const size_t localColumns,
+                     const size_t nextLocalRow, const size_t nextLocalColumn,
+                     const unsigned int k) noexcept {
+    if (localColumns == 0U) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    if (nextLocalRow < localRows) {
+        unsigned int* const distRow =
+            dist.data() + nextLocalRow * localColumns;
+        unsigned int* const pathRow =
+            path.data() + nextLocalRow * localColumns;
+        updateRange(distRow, pathRow, pivotColumn[nextLocalRow], pivotRow.data(),
+                    0U, localColumns, k);
+    }
+
+    if (nextLocalColumn < localColumns) {
+        for (size_t row = 0; row < localRows; ++row) {
+            if (row == nextLocalRow) {
+                continue;
+            }
+            unsigned int& oldDistance =
+                dist[idx2(row, nextLocalColumn, localColumns)];
+            const unsigned int newDistance =
+                pivotColumn[row] + pivotRow[nextLocalColumn];
+            if (newDistance < oldDistance) {
+                oldDistance = newDistance;
+                path[idx2(row, nextLocalColumn, localColumns)] = k;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void preparePivot(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                  std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotRow,
+                  std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotColumn,
+                  const size_t k, const size_t rowStart,
+                  const size_t localRows, const size_t columnStart,
+                  const size_t localColumns) {
+    if (localColumns != 0U && k >= rowStart &&
+        k < rowStart + localRows) {
+        const unsigned int* const source =
+            dist.data() + (k - rowStart) * localColumns;
+        std::copy_n(source, localColumns, pivotRow.data());
+    }
+    if (k >= columnStart && k < columnStart + localColumns) {
+        const size_t localColumn = k - columnStart;
+        for (size_t row = 0; row < localRows; ++row) {
+            pivotColumn[row] = dist[idx2(row, localColumn, localColumns)];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void startPivotBroadcast(std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotRow,
+                         std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotColumn,
+                         const size_t k, const size_t numNodes,
+                         const int processRows, const int processColumns,
+                         MPI_Comm columnComm, MPI_Comm rowComm,
+                         MPI_Request requests[2]) {
+    const int pivotProcessRow = blockOwner(k, numNodes, processRows);
+    const int pivotProcessColumn = blockOwner(k, numNodes, processColumns);
+    MPI_Ibcast(pivotRow.data(), static_cast<int>(pivotRow.size()), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+               pivotProcessRow, columnComm, &requests[0]);
+    MPI_Ibcast(pivotColumn.data(), static_cast<int>(pivotColumn.size()),
+               MPI_UNSIGNED, pivotProcessColumn, rowComm, &requests[1]);
+}
+
+void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
+                   const size_t numNodes, const size_t rowStart,
+                   const size_t localRows, const size_t columnStart,
+                   const size_t localColumns, const int processRows,
+                   const int processColumns, MPI_Comm columnComm,
+                   MPI_Comm rowComm) {
+    if (numNodes == 0U) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotRows[2] = {
+        std::vector<unsigned int>(localColumns),
+        std::vector<unsigned int>(localColumns)};
+    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotColumns[2] = {
+        std::vector<unsigned int>(localRows),
+        std::vector<unsigned int>(localRows)};
+    MPI_Request requests[2][2];
+
+    int slot = 0;
+    preparePivot(dist, pivotRows[slot], pivotColumns[slot], 0U, rowStart,
+                 localRows, columnStart, localColumns);
+    startPivotBroadcast(pivotRows[slot], pivotColumns[slot], 0U, numNodes,
+                        processRows, processColumns, columnComm, rowComm,
+                        requests[slot]);
+
     for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
-        // For each source node i
-        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
-            // For each destination node j
-            for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
-                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
-                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
-                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
-                
-                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distKJ;
-                
-                if (newDist < distIJ) {
-                    dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = newDist;
-                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
+        MPI_Waitall(2, requests[slot], MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+
+        if (k + 1U < numNodes) {
+            const size_t nextK = k + 1U;
+            const size_t nextLocalRow =
+                nextK >= rowStart && nextK < rowStart + localRows
+                    ? nextK - rowStart
+                    : localRows;
+            const size_t nextLocalColumn =
+                nextK >= columnStart && nextK < columnStart + localColumns
+                    ? nextK - columnStart
+                    : localColumns;
+
+            // Update the row and column needed by iteration k+1 first.  Their
+            // broadcasts can then progress while the rest of this block is
+            // computed.
+            updateNextPivot(dist, path, pivotRows[slot], pivotColumns[slot],
+                            localRows, localColumns, nextLocalRow,
+                            nextLocalColumn, static_cast<unsigned int>(k));
+
+            const int nextSlot = slot ^ 1;
+            preparePivot(dist, pivotRows[nextSlot], pivotColumns[nextSlot],
+                         nextK, rowStart, localRows, columnStart, localColumns);
+            startPivotBroadcast(pivotRows[nextSlot], pivotColumns[nextSlot],
+                                nextK, numNodes, processRows, processColumns,
+                                columnComm, rowComm, requests[nextSlot]);
+
+            updateBlock(dist, path, pivotRows[slot], pivotColumns[slot],
+                        localRows, localColumns, nextLocalRow, nextLocalColumn,
+                        static_cast<unsigned int>(k));
+            slot = nextSlot;
+        } else {
+            updateBlock(dist, path, pivotRows[slot], pivotColumns[slot],
+                        localRows, localColumns, localRows, localColumns,
+                        static_cast<unsigned int>(k));
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+std::vector<unsigned int> gatherDistanceMatrix(
+    const std::vector<unsigned int>& localDist, const size_t numNodes,
+    const int worldRank, const int worldSize, const int processRows,
+    const int processColumns, MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr int gatherTag = 417;
+    std::vector<unsigned int> result;
+    if (worldRank == 0) {
+        result.resize(numNodes * numNodes);
+    }
+
+    for (int sender = 0; sender < worldSize; ++sender) {
+        const int senderRow = sender / processColumns;
+        const int senderColumn = sender % processColumns;
+        const size_t senderRows = blockSize(numNodes, senderRow, processRows);
+        const size_t senderColumns =
+            blockSize(numNodes, senderColumn, processColumns);
+        const size_t senderRowStart =
+            blockStart(numNodes, senderRow, processRows);
+        const size_t senderColumnStart =
+            blockStart(numNodes, senderColumn, processColumns);
+
+        if (senderRows == 0U || senderColumns == 0U) {
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        const size_t rowsPerMessage =
+            static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX) / senderColumns;
+        for (size_t rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < senderRows;
+             rowOffset += rowsPerMessage) {
+            const size_t messageRows =
+                std::min(rowsPerMessage, senderRows - rowOffset);
+            const int messageElements =
+                static_cast<int>(messageRows * senderColumns);
+
+            if (worldRank == sender) {
+                if (sender == 0) {
+                    for (size_t row = 0; row < messageRows; ++row) {
+                        const unsigned int* const source =
+                            localDist.data() +
+                            (rowOffset + row) * senderColumns;
+                        unsigned int* const destination =
+                            result.data() +
+                            (senderRowStart + rowOffset + row) * numNodes +
+                            senderColumnStart;
+                        std::copy_n(source, senderColumns, destination);
+                    }
+                } else {
+                    MPI_Send(localDist.data() + rowOffset * senderColumns,
+                             messageElements, MPI_UNSIGNED, 0, gatherTag,
+                             communicator);
                 }
+            } else if (worldRank == 0) {
+                const int globalSizes[2] = {static_cast<int>(numNodes),
+                                            static_cast<int>(numNodes)};
+                const int subSizes[2] = {static_cast<int>(messageRows),
+                                         static_cast<int>(senderColumns)};
+                const int starts[2] = {
+                    static_cast<int>(senderRowStart + rowOffset),
+                    static_cast<int>(senderColumnStart)};
+                MPI_Datatype receiveType;
+                MPI_Type_create_subarray(2, globalSizes, subSizes, starts,
+                                         MPI_ORDER_C, MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                                         &receiveType);
+                MPI_Type_commit(&receiveType);
+                MPI_Recv(result.data(), 1, receiveType, sender, gatherTag,
+                         communicator, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                MPI_Type_free(&receiveType);
             }
         }
     }
+
+    return result;
 }

The full 512-node result also matches the original bit-for-bit (`0998f1bc88b15e85`) on four MPI ranks, and the optimized inner update is being vectorized by the compiler. The implementation shows roughly 3.3× speedup on four local ranks for the default size in this environment; I’m finishing with a clean-build check and confirming that only the two existing files were changed.

floydwarshall.cpp
     1	#include <mpi.h>
     2	
     3	#include <algorithm>

     5	#include <climits>
     6	#include <cstdint>
     7	#include <cstdio>
     8	#include <cstdlib>
     9	#include <cstring>
    10	#include <limits>
    11	#include <stdexcept>
    12	#include <vector>
    13	
    14	#include "../common/results_output.hpp"
    15	
    16	constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
    17	constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;
    18	
    19	// Index calculation for a flattened, row-major 2D array.
    20	inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t row, const size_t column,
    21	                             const size_t rowLength) noexcept {
    22	    return row * rowLength + column;
    23	}
    24	
    25	inline size_t blockSize(const size_t n, const int coordinate,
    26	                        const int parts) noexcept {
    27	    const size_t base = n / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
    28	    const size_t remainder = n % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
    29	    return base + (static_cast<size_t>(coordinate) < remainder ? 1U : 0U);
    30	}
    31	
    32	inline size_t blockStart(const size_t n, const int coordinate,
    33	                         const int parts) noexcept {
    34	    const size_t base = n / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
    35	    const size_t remainder = n % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
    36	    return static_cast<size_t>(coordinate) * base +
    37	           std::min(static_cast<size_t>(coordinate), remainder);
    38	}
    39	
    40	inline int blockOwner(const size_t index, const size_t n,
    41	                      const int parts) noexcept {
    42	    const size_t base = n / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
    43	    const size_t remainder = n % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
    44	    const size_t largeBlockElements = (base + 1U) * remainder;
    45	
    46	    if (index < largeBlockElements) {
    47	        return static_cast<int>(index / (base + 1U));
    48	    }
    49	    return static_cast<int>(remainder +
    50	                            (index - largeBlockElements) / base);
    51	}
    52	
    53	// glibc rand_r advances its 32-bit LCG three times per returned value.  Jumping
    54	// that LCG ahead lets every process initialize its own matrix block while
    55	// reproducing exactly the single-process benchmark's seed-42 input matrix.
    56	unsigned int advanceRandomSeed(const unsigned int initialSeed,
    57	                               const uint64_t randomValues) noexcept {
    58	    uint64_t delta = randomValues * 3U;
    59	    uint32_t accumulatedMultiplier = 1U;
    60	    uint32_t accumulatedIncrement = 0U;
    61	    uint32_t currentMultiplier = 1103515245U;
    62	    uint32_t currentIncrement = 12345U;
    63	
    64	    while (delta != 0U) {
    65	        if ((delta & 1U) != 0U) {
    66	            accumulatedMultiplier = static_cast<uint32_t>(
    67	                static_cast<uint64_t>(accumulatedMultiplier) * currentMultiplier);
    68	            accumulatedIncrement = static_cast<uint32_t>(
    69	                static_cast<uint64_t>(accumulatedIncrement) * currentMultiplier +
    70	                currentIncrement);
    71	        }
    72	        currentIncrement = static_cast<uint32_t>(
    73	            (static_cast<uint64_t>(currentMultiplier) + 1U) * currentIncrement);
    74	        currentMultiplier = static_cast<uint32_t>(
    75	            static_cast<uint64_t>(currentMultiplier) * currentMultiplier);
    76	        delta >>= 1U;
    77	    }
    78	
    79	    return static_cast<unsigned int>(
    80	        static_cast<uint32_t>(
    81	            static_cast<uint64_t>(accumulatedMultiplier) * initialSeed +
    82	            accumulatedIncrement));
    83	}
    84	
    85	void initializeLocalMatrices(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
    86	                             std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
    87	                             const size_t numNodes, const size_t rowStart,
    88	                             const size_t localRows, const size_t columnStart,
    89	                             const size_t localColumns,
    90	                             const unsigned int rangeMin,
    91	                             const unsigned int rangeMax) {
    92	    if (localRows == 0U || localColumns == 0U) {
    93	        return;
    94	    }
    95	
    96	    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;
    97	    const size_t firstElement = rowStart * numNodes + columnStart;
    98	    unsigned int seed = advanceRandomSeed(42U, firstElement);
    99	
   100	    for (size_t localRow = 0; localRow < localRows; ++localRow) {
   101	        const size_t globalRow = rowStart + localRow;
   102	        unsigned int* const distRow = dist.data() + localRow * localColumns;
   103	        unsigned int* const pathRow = path.data() + localRow * localColumns;
   104	
   105	        for (size_t localColumn = 0; localColumn < localColumns; ++localColumn) {
   106	            distRow[localColumn] = rangeMin + static_cast<unsigned int>(
   107	                range * rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX));
   108	            pathRow[localColumn] = static_cast<unsigned int>(globalRow);
   109	        }
   110	
   111	        if (globalRow >= columnStart &&
   112	            globalRow < columnStart + localColumns) {
   113	            distRow[globalRow - columnStart] = 0U;
   114	        }
   115	
   116	        // Move from the end of this local row segment to the same column in
   117	        // the next global row.
   118	        if (localRow + 1U < localRows) {
   119	            seed = advanceRandomSeed(seed, numNodes - localColumns);
   120	        }
   120	        }
   121	    }
   121	    }
   122	}
   122	}
   123	
   123	
   124	inline void updateRange(unsigned int* const distRow,
   124	inline void updateRange(unsigned int* const distRow,
   125	                        unsigned int* const pathRow,
   125	                        unsigned int* const pathRow,
   126	                        const unsigned int distanceToK,
   127	                        const unsigned int* const pivotRow,
   128	                        const size_t begin, const size_t end,
   129	                        const unsigned int k) noexcept {
   130	#if defined(__GNUC__)
   131	#pragma GCC ivdep
   132	#endif
   133	    for (size_t column = begin; column < end; ++column) {
   134	        const unsigned int newDistance = distanceToK + pivotRow[column];
   135	        if (newDistance < distRow[column]) {
   136	            distRow[column] = newDistance;
   137	            pathRow[column] = k;
   138	        }
   139	    }
   140	}
   141	
   142	void updateBlock(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
   143	                 std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
   144	                 const std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotRow,
   145	                 const std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotColumn,
   146	                 const size_t localRows, const size_t localColumns,
   147	                 const size_t excludedRow, const size_t excludedColumn,
   148	                 const unsigned int k) noexcept {
   149	    if (localColumns == 0U) {
   150	        return;
   151	    }
   152	
   153	    for (size_t row = 0; row < localRows; ++row) {
   154	        if (row == excludedRow) {
   155	            continue;
   156	        }
   157	
   158	        unsigned int* const distRow = dist.data() + row * localColumns;
   159	        unsigned int* const pathRow = path.data() + row * localColumns;
   160	        const unsigned int distanceToK = pivotColumn[row];
   161	
   162	        if (excludedColumn < localColumns) {
   163	            updateRange(distRow, pathRow, distanceToK, pivotRow.data(), 0U,
   164	                        excludedColumn, k);
   165	            updateRange(distRow, pathRow, distanceToK, pivotRow.data(),
   166	                        excludedColumn + 1U, localColumns, k);
   167	        } else {
   168	            updateRange(distRow, pathRow, distanceToK, pivotRow.data(), 0U,
   169	                        localColumns, k);
   170	        }
   171	    }
   172	}
   173	
   174	void updateNextPivot(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
   175	                     std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
   176	                     const std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotRow,
   177	                     const std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotColumn,
   178	                     const size_t localRows, const size_t localColumns,
   179	                     const size_t nextLocalRow, const size_t nextLocalColumn,
   180	                     const unsigned int k) noexcept {
   181	    if (localColumns == 0U) {
   182	        return;
   183	    }
   184	
   185	    if (nextLocalRow < localRows) {
   186	        unsigned int* const distRow =
   187	            dist.data() + nextLocalRow * localColumns;
   188	        unsigned int* const pathRow =
   189	            path.data() + nextLocalRow * localColumns;
   190	        updateRange(distRow, pathRow, pivotColumn[nextLocalRow], pivotRow.data(),
   191	                    0U, localColumns, k);
   192	    }
   193	
   194	    if (nextLocalColumn < localColumns) {
   195	        for (size_t row = 0; row < localRows; ++row) {
   196	            if (row == nextLocalRow) {
   197	                continue;
   198	            }
   199	            unsigned int& oldDistance =
   200	                dist[idx2(row, nextLocalColumn, localColumns)];
   201	            const unsigned int newDistance =
   202	                pivotColumn[row] + pivotRow[nextLocalColumn];
   203	            if (newDistance < oldDistance) {
   204	                oldDistance = newDistance;
   205	                path[idx2(row, nextLocalColumn, localColumns)] = k;
   206	            }
   207	        }
   208	    }
   209	}
   210	
   211	void preparePivot(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
   212	                  std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotRow,
   213	                  std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotColumn,
   214	                  const size_t k, const size_t rowStart,
   215	                  const size_t localRows, const size_t columnStart,
   216	                  const size_t localColumns) {
   217	    if (localColumns != 0U && k >= rowStart &&
   218	        k < rowStart + localRows) {
   219	        const unsigned int* const source =
   220	            dist.data() + (k - rowStart) * localColumns;
   221	        std::copy_n(source, localColumns, pivotRow.data());
   222	    }
   223	    if (k >= columnStart && k < columnStart + localColumns) {
   224	        const size_t localColumn = k - columnStart;
   225	        for (size_t row = 0; row < localRows; ++row) {
   226	            pivotColumn[row] = dist[idx2(row, localColumn, localColumns)];
   227	        }
   228	    }
   229	}
   230	
   231	void startPivotBroadcast(std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotRow,
   232	                         std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotColumn,
   233	                         const size_t k, const size_t numNodes,
   234	                         const int processRows, const int processColumns,
   235	                         MPI_Comm columnComm, MPI_Comm rowComm,
   236	                         MPI_Request requests[2]) {
   237	    const int pivotProcessRow = blockOwner(k, numNodes, processRows);
   238	    const int pivotProcessColumn = blockOwner(k, numNodes, processColumns);
   239	    MPI_Ibcast(pivotRow.data(), static_cast<int>(pivotRow.size()), MPI_UNSIGNED,
   240	               pivotProcessRow, columnComm, &requests[0]);
   241	    MPI_Ibcast(pivotColumn.data(), static_cast<int>(pivotColumn.size()),
   242	               MPI_UNSIGNED, pivotProcessColumn, rowComm, &requests[1]);
   243	}
   244	
   245	void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
   246	                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
   247	                   const size_t numNodes, const size_t rowStart,
   248	                   const size_t localRows, const size_t columnStart,
   249	                   const size_t localColumns, const int processRows,
   250	                   const int processColumns, MPI_Comm columnComm,
   251	                   MPI_Comm rowComm) {
   252	    if (numNodes == 0U) {
   253	        return;
   254	    }
   255	
   256	    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotRows[2] = {
   257	        std::vector<unsigned int>(localColumns),
   258	        std::vector<unsigned int>(localColumns)};
   259	    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotColumns[2] = {
   260	        std::vector<unsigned int>(localRows),
   261	        std::vector<unsigned int>(localRows)};
   262	    MPI_Request requests[2][2];
   263	
   264	    int slot = 0;
   265	    preparePivot(dist, pivotRows[slot], pivotColumns[slot], 0U, rowStart,
   266	                 localRows, columnStart, localColumns);
   267	    startPivotBroadcast(pivotRows[slot], pivotColumns[slot], 0U, numNodes,
   268	                        processRows, processColumns, columnComm, rowComm,
   269	                        requests[slot]);
   270	
   271	    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
   272	        MPI_Waitall(2, requests[slot], MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
   273	
   274	        if (k + 1U < numNodes) {
   275	            const size_t nextK = k + 1U;
   276	            const size_t nextLocalRow =
   277	                nextK >= rowStart && nextK < rowStart + localRows
   278	                    ? nextK - rowStart
   279	                    : localRows;
   280	            const size_t nextLocalColumn =
   281	                nextK >= columnStart && nextK < columnStart + localColumns
   282	                    ? nextK - columnStart
   283	                    : localColumns;
   284	
   285	            // Update the row and column needed by iteration k+1 first.  Their
   286	            // broadcasts can then progress while the rest of this block is
   287	            // computed.
   288	            updateNextPivot(dist, path, pivotRows[slot], pivotColumns[slot],
   289	                            localRows, localColumns, nextLocalRow,
   290	                            nextLocalColumn, static_cast<unsigned int>(k));
   291	
   292	            const int nextSlot = slot ^ 1;
   293	            preparePivot(dist, pivotRows[nextSlot], pivotColumns[nextSlot],
   294	                         nextK, rowStart, localRows, columnStart, localColumns);
   295	            startPivotBroadcast(pivotRows[nextSlot], pivotColumns[nextSlot],
   296	                                nextK, numNodes, processRows, processColumns,
   297	                                columnComm, rowComm, requests[nextSlot]);
   298	
   299	            updateBlock(dist, path, pivotRows[slot], pivotColumns[slot],
   300	                        localRows, localColumns, nextLocalRow, nextLocalColumn,
   301	                        static_cast<unsigned int>(k));
   302	            slot = nextSlot;
   303	        } else {
   304	            updateBlock(dist, path, pivotRows[slot], pivotColumns[slot],
   305	                        localRows, localColumns, localRows, localColumns,
   306	                        static_cast<unsigned int>(k));
   307	        }
   308	    }
   309	}
   310	
   311	std::vector<unsigned int> gatherDistanceMatrix(
   312	    const std::vector<unsigned int>& localDist, const size_t numNodes,
   313	    const int worldRank, const int worldSize, const int processRows,
   314	    const int processColumns, MPI_Comm communicator) {
   315	    constexpr int gatherTag = 417;
   440	    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);
   441	
   442	    int worldRank = 0;
   443	    int worldSize = 1;
   444	    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldRank);
   445	    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldSize);
   446	
   447	    size_t numNodes = 512;
   448	    bool validate = false;
   449	    bool printResults = false;
   450	    bool showHelp = false;
   451	    bool argumentsValid = true;
   452	
   453	    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
   454	        if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   455	            const bool nodeCountValid = parseNodeCount(argv[++i], numNodes);
   456	            argumentsValid = argumentsValid && nodeCountValid;
   457	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
   458	            validate = true;
   459	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
   460	            printResults = true;
   461	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
   462	            showHelp = true;
   463	        } else {
   464	            if (worldRank == 0) {
   465	                std::printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
   466	            }
   467	            argumentsValid = false;
   468	        }
   469	    }
   470	
   471	    if (showHelp || !argumentsValid) {
   472	        if (worldRank == 0) {
   473	            printUsage(argv[0]);
   474	        }
   475	        MPI_Finalize();
   476	        return argumentsValid ? 0 : 1;
   477	    }
   478	
   479	    if (numNodes > static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX) ||
   480	        (numNodes != 0U &&
   481	         numNodes > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / numNodes)) {
   482	        if (worldRank == 0) {
   483	            std::fprintf(stderr, "The requested matrix dimensions are too large.\n");
   484	        }
   485	        MPI_Finalize();
   486	        return 1;
   487	    }
   488	
   489	    int dimensions[2] = {0, 0};
   490	    MPI_Dims_create(worldSize, 2, dimensions);
   491	    const int processRows = dimensions[0];
   492	    const int processColumns = dimensions[1];
   493	    const int processRow = worldRank / processColumns;
   494	    const int processColumn = worldRank % processColumns;
   495	
   496	    MPI_Comm rowComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;
   497	    MPI_Comm columnComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;
   498	    MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, processRow, processColumn, &rowComm);
   499	    MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, processColumn, processRow, &columnComm);
   500	
   501	    const size_t localRows = blockSize(numNodes, processRow, processRows);
   502	    const size_t localColumns =
   503	        blockSize(numNodes, processColumn, processColumns);
   504	    const size_t rowStart = blockStart(numNodes, processRow, processRows);
   505	    const size_t columnStart =
   506	        blockStart(numNodes, processColumn, processColumns);
   507	
   508	    int returnCode = 0;
   509	    try {
   510	        if (worldRank == 0) {
   511	            std::printf("Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\n");
   512	            std::printf("Number of nodes: %zu\n", numNodes);
   513	            std::printf("Validation: %s\n",
   514	                        validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   515	            std::printf("Initializing graph...\n");
   516	        }
   517	
   518	        std::vector<unsigned int> localDist(localRows * localColumns);
   519	        std::vector<unsigned int> localPath(localRows * localColumns);
   520	        initializeLocalMatrices(localDist, localPath, numNodes, rowStart,
   521	                                localRows, columnStart, localColumns, 1U,
   522	                                MAX_DISTANCE);
   523	
   524	        if (worldRank == 0) {
   525	            std::printf("Computing shortest paths...\n");
   526	        }
   527	        MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   528	        const double start = MPI_Wtime();
   529	
   530	        floydWarshall(localDist, localPath, numNodes, rowStart, localRows,
   531	                      columnStart, localColumns, processRows, processColumns,
   532	                      columnComm, rowComm);
   533	
   534	        const double localElapsed = MPI_Wtime() - start;
   535	        double elapsed = 0.0;
   536	        MPI_Reduce(&localElapsed, &elapsed, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0,
   537	                   MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   538	
   539	        if (worldRank == 0) {
   540	            const long long durationMs =
   541	                static_cast<long long>(elapsed * 1000.0);
   542	            std::printf("Computation time: %lld ms\n", durationMs);
   543	            const double operations = static_cast<double>(numNodes) *
   544	                                      static_cast<double>(numNodes) *
   545	                                      static_cast<double>(numNodes);
   546	            const double gops = operations / elapsed / 1.0e9;
   547	            std::printf("Performance: %.3f GOPS\n", gops);
   548	        }
   549	
   550	        std::vector<unsigned int> distanceMatrix;
   551	        if (printResults || validate) {
   552	            distanceMatrix = gatherDistanceMatrix(
   553	                localDist, numNodes, worldRank, worldSize, processRows,
   554	                processColumns, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   555	        }
   556	
   557	        if (worldRank == 0 && printResults) {
   558	            print_results_int(distanceMatrix, "DistanceMatrix");
   559	        }
   560	
   561	        int valid = 1;
   562	        if (validate) {
   563	            if (worldRank == 0) {
   564	                std::printf("Validating result...\n");
   565	                valid = validateResult(distanceMatrix, numNodes) ? 1 : 0;
   566	                std::printf("Validation: %s\n", valid != 0 ? "PASSED" : "FAILED");
   567	            }
   568	            MPI_Bcast(&valid, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   569	            returnCode = valid != 0 ? 0 : 1;
   570	        }
   571	    } catch (const std::exception& exception) {
   572	        std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d failed: %s\n", worldRank,
   573	                     exception.what());
   574	        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
   575	        returnCode = 1;
   576	    }
   577	
   578	    MPI_Comm_free(&columnComm);
   579	    MPI_Comm_free(&rowComm);
   580	    MPI_Finalize();
   581	    return returnCode;
   582	}

+// Index calculation for a flattened, row-major 2D array.
+inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t row, const size_t column,
+                             const size_t rowLength) noexcept {
+    return row * rowLength + column;
+}
+
+inline size_t blockSize(const size_t n, const int coordinate,
+                        const int parts) noexcept {
+    const size_t base = n / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t remainder = n % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    return base + (static_cast<size_t>(coordinate) < remainder ? 1U : 0U);
+}
+
+inline size_t blockStart(const size_t n, const int coordinate,
+                         const int parts) noexcept {
+    const size_t base = n / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t remainder = n % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    return static_cast<size_t>(coordinate) * base +
+           std::min(static_cast<size_t>(coordinate), remainder);
+}
+
+inline int blockOwner(const size_t index, const size_t n,
+                      const int parts) noexcept {
+    const size_t base = n / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t remainder = n % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t largeBlockElements = (base + 1U) * remainder;
+
+    if (index < largeBlockElements) {
+        return static_cast<int>(index / (base + 1U));
+    }
+    return static_cast<int>(remainder +
+                            (index - largeBlockElements) / base);
+}
+
+// glibc rand_r advances its 32-bit LCG three times per returned value.  Jumping
+// that LCG ahead lets every process initialize its own matrix block while
+// reproducing exactly the single-process benchmark's seed-42 input matrix.
+unsigned int advanceRandomSeed(const unsigned int initialSeed,
+                               const uint64_t randomValues) noexcept {
+    uint64_t delta = randomValues * 3U;
+    uint32_t accumulatedMultiplier = 1U;
+    uint32_t accumulatedIncrement = 0U;
+    uint32_t currentMultiplier = 1103515245U;
+    uint32_t currentIncrement = 12345U;
+
+    while (delta != 0U) {
+        if ((delta & 1U) != 0U) {
+            accumulatedMultiplier = static_cast<uint32_t>(
+                static_cast<uint64_t>(accumulatedMultiplier) * currentMultiplier);
+            accumulatedIncrement = static_cast<uint32_t>(
+                static_cast<uint64_t>(accumulatedIncrement) * currentMultiplier +
+                currentIncrement);
+        }
+        currentIncrement = static_cast<uint32_t>(
+            (static_cast<uint64_t>(currentMultiplier) + 1U) * currentIncrement);
+        currentMultiplier = static_cast<uint32_t>(
+            static_cast<uint64_t>(currentMultiplier) * currentMultiplier);
+        delta >>= 1U;
+    }
+
+    return static_cast<unsigned int>(
+        static_cast<uint32_t>(
+            static_cast<uint64_t>(accumulatedMultiplier) * initialSeed +
+            accumulatedIncrement));
 }

-void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,

+void updateNextPivot(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                     std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
+                     const std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotRow,
+                     const std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotColumn,
+                     const size_t localRows, const size_t localColumns,
+                     const size_t nextLocalRow, const size_t nextLocalColumn,
+                     const unsigned int k) noexcept {
+    if (localColumns == 0U) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    if (nextLocalRow < localRows) {
+        unsigned int* const distRow =
+            dist.data() + nextLocalRow * localColumns;
+        unsigned int* const pathRow =
+            path.data() + nextLocalRow * localColumns;
+        updateRange(distRow, pathRow, pivotColumn[nextLocalRow], pivotRow.data(),
+                    0U, localColumns, k);
+    }
+
+    if (nextLocalColumn < localColumns) {
+        for (size_t row = 0; row < localRows; ++row) {
+            if (row == nextLocalRow) {
+                continue;
+            }
+            unsigned int& oldDistance =
+                dist[idx2(row, nextLocalColumn, localColumns)];
+            const unsigned int newDistance =
+                pivotColumn[row] + pivotRow[nextLocalColumn];
+            if (newDistance < oldDistance) {
+                oldDistance = newDistance;
+                path[idx2(row, nextLocalColumn, localColumns)] = k;
+            }
+        }

+void preparePivot(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                  std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotRow,
+                  std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotColumn,
+                  const size_t k, const size_t rowStart,
+                  const size_t localRows, const size_t columnStart,
+                  const size_t localColumns) {
+    if (localColumns != 0U && k >= rowStart &&
+        k < rowStart + localRows) {
+        const unsigned int* const source =
+            dist.data() + (k - rowStart) * localColumns;
+        std::copy_n(source, localColumns, pivotRow.data());
+    }
+    if (k >= columnStart && k < columnStart + localColumns) {
+        const size_t localColumn = k - columnStart;
+        for (size_t row = 0; row < localRows; ++row) {
+            pivotColumn[row] = dist[idx2(row, localColumn, localColumns)];
+        }
+    }
+}

+void startPivotBroadcast(std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotRow,

+                         MPI_Comm columnComm, MPI_Comm rowComm,
+                         MPI_Request requests[2]) {
+    const int pivotProcessRow = blockOwner(k, numNodes, processRows);
+    const int pivotProcessColumn = blockOwner(k, numNodes, processColumns);
+    MPI_Ibcast(pivotRow.data(), static_cast<int>(pivotRow.size()), MPI_UNSIGNED,

+    MPI_Ibcast(pivotColumn.data(), static_cast<int>(pivotColumn.size()),
+               MPI_UNSIGNED, pivotProcessColumn, rowComm, &requests[1]);

-                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;

+
+        if (k + 1U < numNodes) {
+            const size_t nextK = k + 1U;
+            const size_t nextLocalRow =
+                nextK >= rowStart && nextK < rowStart + localRows
+                    ? nextK - rowStart
+                    : localRows;
+            const size_t nextLocalColumn =
+                nextK >= columnStart && nextK < columnStart + localColumns
+                    ? nextK - columnStart
+                    : localColumns;
+
+            // Update the row and column needed by iteration k+1 first.  Their
+            // broadcasts can then progress while the rest of this block is
+            // computed.
+            updateNextPivot(dist, path, pivotRows[slot], pivotColumns[slot],
+                            localRows, localColumns, nextLocalRow,
+                            nextLocalColumn, static_cast<unsigned int>(k));
+
+            const int nextSlot = slot ^ 1;
+            preparePivot(dist, pivotRows[nextSlot], pivotColumns[nextSlot],
+                         nextK, rowStart, localRows, columnStart, localColumns);
+            startPivotBroadcast(pivotRows[nextSlot], pivotColumns[nextSlot],
+                                nextK, numNodes, processRows, processColumns,
+                                columnComm, rowComm, requests[nextSlot]);
+
+            updateBlock(dist, path, pivotRows[slot], pivotColumns[slot],
+                        localRows, localColumns, nextLocalRow, nextLocalColumn,
+                        static_cast<unsigned int>(k));
+            slot = nextSlot;
+        } else {
+            updateBlock(dist, path, pivotRows[slot], pivotColumns[slot],
+                        localRows, localColumns, localRows, localColumns,
+                        static_cast<unsigned int>(k));
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+std::vector<unsigned int> gatherDistanceMatrix(
+    const std::vector<unsigned int>& localDist, const size_t numNodes,
+    const int worldRank, const int worldSize, const int processRows,
+    const int processColumns, MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr int gatherTag = 417;
+    std::vector<unsigned int> result;
+    if (worldRank == 0) {
+        result.resize(numNodes * numNodes);
+    }
+
+    for (int sender = 0; sender < worldSize; ++sender) {
+        const int senderRow = sender / processColumns;
+        const int senderColumn = sender % processColumns;
+        const size_t senderRows = blockSize(numNodes, senderRow, processRows);
+        const size_t senderColumns =
+            blockSize(numNodes, senderColumn, processColumns);
+        const size_t senderRowStart =
+            blockStart(numNodes, senderRow, processRows);
+        const size_t senderColumnStart =
+            blockStart(numNodes, senderColumn, processColumns);
+
+        if (senderRows == 0U || senderColumns == 0U) {
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        const size_t rowsPerMessage =
+            static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX) / senderColumns;
+        for (size_t rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < senderRows;
+             rowOffset += rowsPerMessage) {
+            const size_t messageRows =
+                std::min(rowsPerMessage, senderRows - rowOffset);
+            const int messageElements =
+                static_cast<int>(messageRows * senderColumns);
+
+            if (worldRank == sender) {
+                if (sender == 0) {
+                    for (size_t row = 0; row < messageRows; ++row) {
+                        const unsigned int* const source =
+                            localDist.data() +
+                            (rowOffset + row) * senderColumns;
+                        unsigned int* const destination =
+                            result.data() +
+                            (senderRowStart + rowOffset + row) * numNodes +
+                            senderColumnStart;
+                        std::copy_n(source, senderColumns, destination);
+                    }
+                } else {
+                    MPI_Send(localDist.data() + rowOffset * senderColumns,
+                             messageElements, MPI_UNSIGNED, 0, gatherTag,
+                             communicator);
                 }
+            } else if (worldRank == 0) {
+                const int globalSizes[2] = {static_cast<int>(numNodes),
+                                            static_cast<int>(numNodes)};
+                const int subSizes[2] = {static_cast<int>(messageRows),
+                                         static_cast<int>(senderColumns)};
+                const int starts[2] = {
+                    static_cast<int>(senderRowStart + rowOffset),
+                    static_cast<int>(senderColumnStart)};
+                MPI_Datatype receiveType;
+                MPI_Type_create_subarray(2, globalSizes, subSizes, starts,
+                                         MPI_ORDER_C, MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                                         &receiveType);
+                MPI_Type_commit(&receiveType);
+                MPI_Recv(result.data(), 1, receiveType, sender, gatherTag,
+                         communicator, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                MPI_Type_free(&receiveType);
             }
         }
     }
+
+    return result;
 }

+bool parseNodeCount(const char* text, size_t& value) noexcept {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' || text[0] == '-' ||
+        parsed > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max()) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
 }

codex
Implemented MPI-distributed Floyd–Warshall while preserving the `floydwarshall` executable name.

Key changes:

- 2D MPI process-grid matrix distribution.
- Nonblocking pivot row/column broadcasts overlapped with computation.
- Distributed deterministic initialization matching the original seed-42 matrix.
- Vectorized local update loops.
- Full matrix gathered only for `-r` or `-v`.
- MPI required unconditionally in [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/floydwarshall_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_mpi_r1/floydwarshall/CMakeLists.txt:8).
- Core implementation in [floydwarshall.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/floydwarshall_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_mpi_r1/floydwarshall/floydwarshall.cpp:245).

Verification completed:

- Clean release build succeeds without warnings.
- Validation passes across 1–9 MPI ranks and uneven process grids.
- Default 512-node output matches the original exact hash: `0998f1bc88b15e85`.
- UBSan edge-case testing passed, including more ranks than graph nodes.
- No new files or dependencies were added beyond required MPI.

 constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;

-// Index calculation for flattened 2D array

-    return j * n + i;
+// Index calculation for a flattened, row-major 2D array.
+inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t row, const size_t column,
+                             const size_t rowLength) noexcept {
+    return row * rowLength + column;
+}
+
+inline size_t blockSize(const size_t n, const int coordinate,
+                        const int parts) noexcept {
+    const size_t base = n / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t remainder = n % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    return base + (static_cast<size_t>(coordinate) < remainder ? 1U : 0U);
+}
+
+inline size_t blockStart(const size_t n, const int coordinate,
+                         const int parts) noexcept {
+    const size_t base = n / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t remainder = n % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    return static_cast<size_t>(coordinate) * base +
+           std::min(static_cast<size_t>(coordinate), remainder);
+}
+
+inline int blockOwner(const size_t index, const size_t n,
+                      const int parts) noexcept {
+    const size_t base = n / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t remainder = n % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t largeBlockElements = (base + 1U) * remainder;
+
+    if (index < largeBlockElements) {
+        return static_cast<int>(index / (base + 1U));
+    }
+    return static_cast<int>(remainder +
+                            (index - largeBlockElements) / base);
+}
+
+// glibc rand_r advances its 32-bit LCG three times per returned value.  Jumping
+// that LCG ahead lets every process initialize its own matrix block while
+// reproducing exactly the single-process benchmark's seed-42 input matrix.
+unsigned int advanceRandomSeed(const unsigned int initialSeed,
+                               const uint64_t randomValues) noexcept {
+    uint64_t delta = randomValues * 3U;
+    uint32_t accumulatedMultiplier = 1U;
+    uint32_t accumulatedIncrement = 0U;
+    uint32_t currentMultiplier = 1103515245U;
+    uint32_t currentIncrement = 12345U;
+
+    while (delta != 0U) {
+        if ((delta & 1U) != 0U) {
+            accumulatedMultiplier = static_cast<uint32_t>(
+                static_cast<uint64_t>(accumulatedMultiplier) * currentMultiplier);
+            accumulatedIncrement = static_cast<uint32_t>(
+                static_cast<uint64_t>(accumulatedIncrement) * currentMultiplier +
+                currentIncrement);
+        }
+        currentIncrement = static_cast<uint32_t>(
+            (static_cast<uint64_t>(currentMultiplier) + 1U) * currentIncrement);
+        currentMultiplier = static_cast<uint32_t>(
+            static_cast<uint64_t>(currentMultiplier) * currentMultiplier);
+        delta >>= 1U;
+    }
+
+    return static_cast<unsigned int>(
+        static_cast<uint32_t>(
+            static_cast<uint64_t>(accumulatedMultiplier) * initialSeed +
+            accumulatedIncrement));
 }

+        unsigned int* const distRow = dist.data() + localRow * localColumns;
+        unsigned int* const pathRow = path.data() + localRow * localColumns;
+

+                range * rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX));

     }

+inline void updateRange(unsigned int* const distRow,
+                        unsigned int* const pathRow,
+                        const unsigned int distanceToK,
+                        const unsigned int* const pivotRow,
+                        const size_t begin, const size_t end,
+                        const unsigned int k) noexcept {
+#if defined(__GNUC__)
+#pragma GCC ivdep
+#endif
+    for (size_t column = begin; column < end; ++column) {
+        const unsigned int newDistance = distanceToK + pivotRow[column];
+        if (newDistance < distRow[column]) {
+            distRow[column] = newDistance;
+            pathRow[column] = k;
+        }
     }
 }

+                 const std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotRow,

-void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
-                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
